[Table_Title] 
假期扰动 PMI走势，内外需双双放缓

[Table_ReportType] 
——10月PMI数据点评

[Table_ReportDate] 2026年11月02日

[Table_Summary] ⚫ 事件： 10月中采制造业PMI为 49.3%，较上月下跌0.8个
百分点，非制造业PMI由50.2%下跌至49.7%。 
⚫ 制造业PMI环比季节性回落，中小企业景气跌幅较大。PMI
再度降至临界值下方，工作天数减少带来一定影响。中、小
型企业PMI回落幅度较大，均处于临近值下方，大型企业
PMI小幅回落，仍处于景气区间。 
⚫ 产需双双回落，进出口景气度降温，价格指标景气度回落。
生产指数降至临界值下方，新订单指数下跌，生产回落快于
需求。新出口订单、进口指数下跌，而欧美日等制造业PMI
景气度整体保持旺盛。企业采购意愿下滑，生产放缓影响企
业采购需求。纺织、有色金属、非金属矿等行业生产、新订
单偏弱。原材料价格回落幅度大于出厂价格跌幅，中下游企
业成本压力边际缓解，有色金属板块价格跌幅较大，能耗产
业链价格维持高景气。原材料库存累积、产成品库存去化的
趋势持续，主动补库存周期尚不稳固。 
⚫ 高技术、装备制造高位放缓，消费品、基础原材料、钢铁行
业景气度下滑。新经济景气度高位回落，但仍处景气区间，
EPMI大幅拉升，供需持续好转，秋季旺季特征明显，采购
价格持续上行，但速度放缓。SMI中枢上行，中上游制造业
显著修复。消费品行业PMI重回荣枯线下方，双节备货拉动
消退后，内需接续效应不足。兰格钢铁PMI下滑幅度较大，
销售价格和采购成本大幅回落，供需均转弱。 
⚫ 建筑业为非制造业PMI主要拖累，服务业PMI处于临界点。
非制造业PMI新订单和新出口订单指数下滑幅度较大，需求
持续偏弱，价格指标回落。其中，建筑业PMI下滑至临界值
下方，工作日减少带来一定影响，新订单指数跌幅较大，建
筑业销售价格上行，投入品价格回落，钢材价格下跌形成一
定拖累。服务业PMI小幅放缓，新订单指数跌幅较大，价格
指数回落，仍处景气区间，十一长假给价格带来支撑。 
⚫ 季节性因素干扰PMI表现，政策扶持下四季度经济仍有支
撑。10月受长假和外部扰动等影响，PMI环比承压，内外需
均放缓。BCI企业利润、销售前瞻指数小幅回落，销售前瞻
指数维持在60以上，仍具韧性，油价上行给利润带来一定
压力，融资环境指数小幅上行。随着财政资金逐步落地，有
望加快形成实物工作量，带动基建、制造业投资好转。 
⚫ 风险因素：地缘政治风险发展超预期，政策出台和落地不及
预期，外需回落幅度超预期。

采矿业为利润主要拉动，企业库存持续累积 
财政支出有望继续提速

万联证券研究所 www.wlzq.cn 第 3 页 共 7 页  
事件 
2026年10月，中采制造业PMI为49.3%， 较上月下跌0.8个百分点， 非制造业PMI为49.7%，
较上月下跌0.5个百分点，综合PMI为49.7%，环比下跌1个百分点。

1    PMI环比季节性回落，产需双双放缓 
制造业PMI环比季节性回落，中小企业景气回落幅度较大。制造业PMI环比回落0.8个
百分点至49.3%，再度降至临界值下方，工作天数减少带来一定影响，财政投放提速，
项目建设给PMI表现形成支撑。从不同规模企业来看，小型企业PMI下行1.4个百分点
至47.5%， 中型企业PMI下行1.3个百分点至48.4%， 大型企业PMI由50.6%回落至50.2%，
仅大型企业处于景气区间，中小企业依然承压。 
高技术、装备制造高位继续回落，战略新兴产业显著回暖，消费品、基础原材料、钢
铁行业景气度下滑。高技术制造业继续下行1.2个百分点至51.3%，装备制造业由51%
回落为50.6%，高端制造业景气度持续回落，但仍处景气区间。基础原材料行业由48%
回落为47.3%。消费品行业下行1.9个百分点至48.8%，重回荣枯线下方，双节备货拉
动消退后，内需接续效应不足。战略性新兴产业PMI（EPMI）由52.3%拉升至58%，达
到年内高点，其中，生产量、产品订货和进口指数上行幅度较大，供需双双好转，秋
季旺季特征明显， 但进口指数仍处荣枯线下方， 采购价格持续上行， 但速度明显放缓，
就业和研发指标也有所上行。光伏经理人指数（SMI）10月中枢较9月上行，中上游制
造业显著修复，下游电站小幅回落。兰格钢铁PMI由49.8%回落为46.9%，下滑幅度较
大，其中，销售价格和采购成本大幅回落，下行至荣枯线下方，销量、总订单、国内
订单也明显回落，出口订单量小幅下滑，仅库存水平环比上行。

图表1：10月制造业PMI环比季节性回落（%） 图表2：高技术制造、装备制造业PMI继续回落（%）

资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所 资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所

口指数环比转为下跌2.2个百分点至47%。采购量下行2.1个百分点至48.9%，生产放缓
影响企业采购需求回落。供货商配送时间由50.1%上行至50.2%。从统计局公布数据来
看，铁路船舶航空航天设备、计算机通信电子设备等行业生产指数和新订单指数均位
于53.0%及以上；纺织、非金属矿物制品、有色金属冶炼及压延加工等行业生产指数
和新订单指数均低于临界点，地产持续偏弱对上游的拖累作用持续，部分有色金属价
格、库存偏高给产需带来一定影响。美国制造业PMI高位边际放缓，但景气度仍强，
日本制造业PMI持续小幅回落，仍处荣枯线上方。德国、英国制造业PMI上行，处于临
界点上方，法国受国内动乱影响，制造业PMI降至荣枯线下方。 
图表3：供需分项环比回落，价格指标下行（%） 图表4：美国制造业PMI小幅回落，景气仍强（%）

资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所 资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所 
价格指标景气度回落，原材料和加工行业剪刀差收窄，有色行业价格下滑为主要拖
累，能化产业链维持高景气。主要原材料购进价格回落4个百分点至56.8%，出厂价格
下行2.3个百分点至51.7%，原材料价格回落幅度大于出厂价格跌幅，中下游企业成本
压力边际缓解。10月，受到宏观流动性收紧预期和基本面供需矛盾等因素影响，有色
金属板块承压，金、银、钴、碳酸锂等有色金属跌幅较大。国际油价持续攀升，石油
煤炭及其他燃料加工、化学纤维及橡胶塑料制品等行业价格指数维持高景气。 
原材料库存累积、产成品库存去化的趋势持续，主动补库存周期尚不稳固。产成品库
存由47.6%下跌至47.5%， 原材料库存上行0.2个百分点至48.4%。原料端价格压力持续，
企业备库需求和价格增长带动原材料库存上行。生产经营活动预期指标由53.8%回落
为53.4%。 
图表5：原材料库存累积，产成品库存去化（%） 图表6：原材料价格和出厂价格价差明显收窄（%）

2    非制造业PMI回落，建筑业为主要拖累 
非制造业PMI回落，需求持续偏弱，价格指标回落。10月非制造业PMI为49.7%，较上
月下跌0.5个百分点。其中，新订单和新出口订单指数下滑幅度较大，持续处于临近
值下方，在手订单指数也有所回落。商务活动指数下滑，降至临近值下方。投入品价
格回落幅度大于销售价格回落幅度。业务活动预期由55.5%回落至54.7%，企业经营预
期下跌，但仍处景气区间。仅从业人员由45.9%小幅上行至46%。 
建筑业PMI下滑至临界值下方，新订单指数下滑幅度较大，工作日减少带来一定影响，
销售价格和投入品价格走势分化。建筑业PMI下跌至48%，上月为50.3%。其中，建筑
业新订单指数下跌5.7个百分点至40%， 建筑业业务活动预期下跌3.1个百分点至52.3%，
建筑业销售价格上行，投入品价格回落，钢材价格下跌形成一定拖累。随着财政资金
落地， “六张网”建设逐步显效，有望带动建筑业回暖。 
服务业PMI处于临界点，新订单指数持续偏弱，价格指数回落。服务业PMI为50%，较
上月下跌0.2个百分点。服务业新订单指数下跌，回落幅度相对最大。服务业投入品
和销售价格均下跌，仍处景气区间，十一长假给价格带来支撑。业务活动预期指数小
幅回落，仍处临界值上方。从统计局公布数据来看，电信广播电视及卫星传输服务、
货币金融服务、保险等行业商务活动指数持续位于55.0%以上的高景气区间；资本市
场服务、房地产等行业商务活动指数低位运行。

图表7：建筑业PMI新订单跌幅较大（%） 图表8：服务业新订单下跌(%)

资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所 资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所

制造业用工需求持平，服务业就业好转，建筑业就业回落。10月制造业PMI从业人员
持平于上月的48.4%。服务业从业人员较上月上行0.5个百分点至46.7%，建筑业从业
人员指数为42%，较上月下跌2.3个百分点，十一长假影响建筑业用工需求，给服务业
就业带来提振。

3    总结与展望 
季节性因素干扰PMI表现，政策扶持下四季度经济仍有支撑。10月受长假和外部扰动
等影响，PMI环比承压，内外需均放缓。BCI企业利润、销售前瞻指数小幅回落，销售
前瞻指数维持在60以上，仍具韧性，油价上行给利润带来一定压力，融资环境指数小
幅上行。随着财政资金逐步落地，有望加快形成实物工作量，带动基建、制造业投资
好转。

图表9：企业利润、销售前瞻指数小幅回落（%） 图表10：企业融资环境指数小幅上行（%）

资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所 资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所

4    风险提示 
地缘政治风险发展超预期，政策出台和落地不及预期，外需回落幅度超预期。

强于大市：未来6个月内行业指数相对大盘涨幅10％以上； 
同步大市：未来6个月内行业指数相对大盘涨幅10％至-10％之间； 
弱于大市：未来6个月内行业指数相对大盘跌幅10％以上。

[Table_StockRankInfo] 公司投资评级 
买入：未来6个月内公司相对大盘涨幅15％以上； 
增持：未来6个月内公司相对大盘涨幅5％至15％； 
观望：未来6个月内公司相对大盘涨幅-5％至5％； 
卖出：未来6个月内公司相对大盘跌幅5％以上。

相对比重建议；投资者买入或者卖出证券的决定取决于个人的实际情况，比如当前的持仓结构以及其他需要考
虑的因素。投资者应阅读整篇报告，以获取比较完整的观点与信息，不应仅仅依靠投资评级来推断结论。

1

國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
企業活動維持擴張，消費信心與房市疲弱，通膨及關稅風險仍存 
經濟 
⚫ 10 月美國綜合 PMI 產出指數初值由 58.4 降至 57.6，企業活動仍強勁擴
張，內需及出口帶動訂單與就業成長；惟油價、關稅及供應鏈瓶頸推升
成本，售價漲幅創 2022 年 8 月以來新高，通膨壓力持續升溫，後續須
關注聯準會政策動向。 
⚫ 9 月美國住宅市場續顯疲弱，建築許可及新屋開工分別年減 3.0%及
7.4%，反映住宅投資動能放緩。儘管多戶住宅許可與完工數回升，但整
體營建活動仍偏弱，後續仍須關注房貸利率、住房負擔能力及營建成本
對市場復甦的影響。 
⚫ 密西根大學公布之 10 月美國消費者信心指數降至 48.0，月減 0.2%、年
減 10.4%，顯示民眾對目前經濟環境仍缺乏信心，雖然對未來景氣的看
法略有改善，但高物價與借貸成本仍讓消費者感到壓力。此外，未來一
年及長期通膨預期分別升至 4.7%及 3.5%，反映民眾仍擔心物價持續上
漲。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 10 月 23 日川普宣布調整加拿大商品 50%關稅清單，10 月 30 日生效，
豁免部分紙製品、機械及包裝材料，並新增地毯、油漆等課稅項目。外
媒認為此舉與期中選舉有關，有助降低部分企業成本，但美加貿易摩擦
仍未化解。 
短評 
⚫ 10 月美國綜合產出指數雖略為下滑 ， 但仍維持高檔 ， 顯示內需與就業持
續支撐企業活動擴張。然而，消費者信心仍處低檔，反映高物價與借貸
成本持續壓抑購買意願，儘管民眾對未來景氣的看法略有改善，但新屋
開工及建築許可同步下滑，顯示消費與住宅投資前景仍面臨挑戰。另一
方面，美國調整部分加拿大商品關稅，雖有助減輕部分企業進口成本，
但雙邊貿易摩擦尚未化解，政策不確定性仍存。

國際經濟金融週報 
兆豐國際商業銀行 徵信處 
115.10.27 
一、 美國—企業活動維持擴張，消費信心與房市疲弱，通膨及關稅風險仍存 
標普全球(S&P Global)公布，2026 年 10 月美國綜合 PMI 產出指數(Composite 
PMI Output Index) 初值由上月的 58.4 降至 57.6，其中服務業採購經理人指數
(Services PMI)由 58.8 降至 57.9，製造業採購經理人指數(Manufacturing PMI)則由
55.9 微升至 56.0，創 2022 年 5 月以來新高，企業活動雖略有放緩，但仍維持強
勁擴張。需求方面，內需穩健及出口改善帶動整體新訂單增速創逾 4 年半新高，
商品出口訂單亦為 2025 年 6 月以來首次增加；企業持續擴增人力，整體就業增
速為 2022 年 6 月以來次高。另一方面，未完成訂單增加、供應商交貨延遲加劇，
加上油價上漲及關稅推升能源、運輸與原物料成本，使企業投入成本維持高檔，
帶動售價漲幅升至 2022 年 8 月以來最高。儘管企業對未來一年營運前景的樂觀
程度略降，但仍處於今年相對高檔。整體而言，美國經濟在內外需及就業支撐下
維持擴張，惟供應鏈瓶頸與成本上升加劇通膨壓力，後續仍須關注物價走勢及聯
準會貨幣政策動向。 
密西根大學公布，2026 年 10 月美國消費者信心指數終值降至 48.0，略低於
上月的 48.1，月減 0.2%、年減 10.4%，並較今年 2 月下降約 15%，顯示消費者信
心持續處於低檔。分項來看，當前經濟狀況指數由 50.9 降至 46.7，月減 8.3%、
年減 20.3%；消費者預期指數則由 46.3 回升至 48.8，月增 5.4%、年減 3.0%，反
映民眾對未來景氣的看法略有改善，但整體信心仍偏弱。調查指出，高利率及購
買負擔壓力使耐久財購買條件下降 18%，拖累當前經濟狀況指數；未來一年企業
景氣預期雖改善約 14%，但仍低於去年同期 16%。通膨預期方面，未來一年通膨
預期由 4.6%升至 4.7%， 長期通膨預期亦由3.4%升至 3.5%， 雙雙連續第二個月攀
升，創今年 5 月以來最高水準。密西根大學指出，2025 年關稅政策衝擊及 2026
年伊朗衝突引發的能源價格上漲，使民眾通膨預期再度升溫，且長期預期已明顯
高於 2019 年及 2024 年水準。整體而言，10 月美國消費者信心大致持平，但高物
價與借貸成本仍壓抑購買意願，加上短期及長期通膨預期持續上升，可能增加物
價回穩的難度，後續仍須關注能源價格、家庭消費及通膨預期變化對聯準會貨幣
政策的影響。

2026 年 9 月美國住宅市場建築許可年化總數降至 140.0 萬戶，月減0.2%、年
減 3.0%，其中單戶住宅建築許可降至 84.5 萬戶，月減 4.0%、年減 4.8%，惟 5 戶
以上多戶住宅建築許可增至 50.3 萬戶，月增 5.9%、年增 1.0%，顯示不同住宅類
型開發規劃表現分歧。新屋開工年化數降至 122.1 萬戶，月減5.2%、年減 7.4%，
其中單戶住宅開工 85.9 萬戶，較去年同期仍增 2.6%，但多戶住宅開工降至 34.9
萬戶，年減25.9%，拖累整體營建動能。施工方面，在建住宅維持128.0 萬戶，與
上月持平但較去年減少 3.3%；完工年化數則回升至 119.2 萬戶，月增2.2%，主要
受多戶住宅完工月增 14.9%帶動，惟整體仍較去年同期減少 14.0%。整體而言，
美國住宅市場呈現建築許可及新屋開工下滑、在建規模持平及完工略有回升的態
勢，反映住宅營建活動仍偏弱，後續仍須關注房貸利率走勢、住房負擔能力及營
建成本變化對住宅市場復甦的影響。 
美國總統川普 10 月 23 日依據《1930 年關稅法》第 338 條簽署公告，調整部
分加拿大商品加徵 50%關稅的適用範圍，並自 10 月 30 日起生效。此次調整取消
膠合板、部分紙製品、機械與冷凍設備、包裝用木箱及紙箱、新鮮與乾燥海藻等
商品的額外關稅，同時新增閥門、地毯、油漆、洗髮精、可可粉、木製棺材、空
調設備及獨木舟等課稅項目，呈現局部鬆綁與新增課稅並行的政策方向。白宮表
示 ， 此舉旨在減輕加拿大貿易措施對美國企業造成的負擔 ， 並維護國內產業利益 。
由於緬因州農業及製造業高度依賴加拿大進口物資，共和黨參議員柯林斯 (Susan 
Collins)積極向白宮爭取關稅豁免 ， 外媒亦將此次調整與11 月 3 日期中選舉連結 。
整體而言，新措施有助降低部分美國企業的進口成本，但美加貿易摩擦尚未完全
化解，後續仍須關注雙邊談判及關稅政策對供應鏈的影響。 
二、歐元區—法國動盪短期內難降溫，料將使央行保持審慎觀望態度 
 歐元區 10 月綜合採購經理人指數(PMI)下降 1.3 點至 51.9，抵銷上月的強勁
增幅，但為連續五個月高於 50 的榮枯線，主要係受到法國大規模抗議活動的影
響，使法國綜合 PMI 下降 2.7 點至 48.4；此外，德國綜合PMI 下降 1 點至 52.8，
亦反映近期通膨壓力增加 ， 使民眾傾向保守消費 。 若以產業區分 ， 歐元區服務業 、
製造業 PMI 分別降 1.3、0.5 點至 51.7、52.9；惟德國製造業 PMI 上升至相當強勁
的 54.2，反映德國政府擴張軍備投資以及基礎建設之需求，持續推動製造業生產
動能。

法國抗爭活動規模逐漸擴大 ， 各大工會與抗議團體已宣布在 10 月 31 日再度
發起大規模遊行，並預告於 11 月 5 日舉行全國性大罷工，抗議主要訴求包括改

數的情況下，法國政府既無法強力通過重大的財政改革，也難以快速推出安撫反
對派的妥協法案或者公共支出。故部分較為悲觀的機構認為此波動盪可能會延續
至 2027 年 4 月的法國總統大選。受法國局勢影響，近期歐元區各國殖利率普遍
呈現上升趨勢，並對景氣產生暫時性的抑制效果。在此背景下，市場預期歐洲央
行在 10 月 28 日的政策會議將採取觀望立場，後續關注歐洲央行是否將透過政策
訊號或流動性工具，為平息法國主權債務利率而採取行動。 
三、日本—貿易逆差及通膨壓力延續，內需仍具韌性 
 9 月出口雖維持強勁 ， 受半導體製造設備及汽車出口增加所帶動 ， 出口額年增
16.7%；惟進口增幅更為顯著，9 月進口額年增 18.9%，其中原油及液化天然氣進
口額分別大增 51.1%及 39.2%，主要反映中東局勢推升能源價格，以及日本尋求
替代能源供應來源的影響。由於進口額高於出口額，9 月貿易逆差達 5,365 億日
圓，為連續第 5 個月呈現逆差；累計 2026 上半財年（4 至 9 月）貿易逆差亦較上
年同期擴大 98.8%至 2.82 兆日圓。整體而言，出口並未明顯轉弱，近期貿易逆差
擴大主要反映能源進口成本攀升，使進口增幅持續高於出口。 
 9 月全國消費者物價指數(CPI)年增 2.1%，即使政府透過能源及教育補貼等措
施抑制物價，通膨仍高於2%，顯示實際通膨動能更為強勁。從物價結構來看，石
油、原物料及工資成本上升已逐步傳導至終端價格，漲價範圍由家具、醫療用品
及休閒用品等商品 ， 進一步擴散至房屋維護 、 公共運輸 、 住宿及外食等服務項目 ，
顯示企業成本轉嫁範圍逐步擴大，通膨壓力亦由商品向服務領域擴散。隨通膨持
續高於 2%且漲價範圍擴大 ， 亦為日本央行進一步推動貨幣政策正常化提供支撐 。 
 10 月綜合 PMI 持平於 52.3，顯示經濟仍維持穩定擴張，其中製造業 PMI 下
降 0.8 點至 53.3，產出指數亦降至 3 月以來最低，主要反映新訂單及出口訂單成
長放緩，加上稀土金屬等原料供應受限，對生產形成一定壓力，惟生產與訂單仍
處擴張區間，尚未顯示製造業景氣明顯轉弱；服務業 PMI 回升 0.7 點至 52.0，新
業務及出口訂單同步改善，顯示服務需求仍具韌性，為支撐景氣的重要動能。另
一方面，能源、薪資及原物料成本居高不下，使投入價格壓力持續偏高，並逐漸

### Page 8

本報告係根據研究資料及各項可靠數據作成，兆豐銀行保留報告內容所有權利，未經本行許可，請勿引
用相關內容。

7

影響企業對未來景氣的信心。 
四、中國—第三季經濟成長率回升，但前景仍較為不利 
2026 年第三季 GDP 年增率初估值為 4.5%，較第二季的 4.3%上揚 0.2 個百分
點；累計 1-9 月 GDP 年增率則為 4.6%。第三季的二、三級產業年增率分別較第
二季上揚 0.4、0.1 個百分點至 3.4%， 其中製造業 、 批發零售業年增率分別達5.9%、
2.3%為主要動能 ， 抵銷建築業年減4.4%等拖累因素影響 。 整體而言 ， 第三季GDP
年增率上揚有部分受到中秋國慶連假落在 9 月末段(去年同期則於 10 月 1 日開
始)，致基期效果發揮部分影響，相關消費動能仍可能會在 10 月出現暫時性的回
落。 
在需求指標方面，(1)1-9 月累計零售額年增率為 1.1%，持平於 1-8 月的累計
年增率。其中 9 月零售額年增率增加 0.7 個百分點至 1.1%，部分受到新手機上市
帶動買氣，9 月通訊器材消費年增率高達 31.1%，加上中秋國慶長假帶動食品消
費年增率增加 1 個百分點至 2.1%所致 ；(2)1-9 月固定資產投資年減幅維持於 7.2%，
反映供應鏈移轉、政府主導去化過剩產能等因素導致民間投資需求急遽萎縮；萎
縮產業包含化工、醫藥、專用設備、汽車製造業、金屬製品業等；(3)1-9 月房地
產開發投資額年減幅由 19.9%擴大至 20.6%、施工面積年減幅為 12.8%，新建商
品房銷售面積年減幅擴大為 13.2%，房市投資、買氣皆持續低迷。 
受到出口維持高度成長帶動，9 月工業生產年增率較 8 月增加 0.3 個百分點
至 5.5%(累計 1-9 月工業生產年增率持平 1-8 月水準為 5.3%)。依產業別來看，9
月主要受到煤礦開採、化工、化纖等產業擴張影響，主因中秋與國慶長假前發電
業充實庫存水位，促使煤炭企業加快復產，加上油價上漲帶動相關中下游供應鏈
增產；惟高科技產品包含 IC、機器人、新能源車等年增率皆有放慢，反映去年中
秋國慶連假皆落在 10 月，使得去年 9 月工作日較多，致基期相對墊高的影響。 
展望未來 ， 儘管政府10 月初宣布釋放 5,500 億元人民幣的地方政府債務結存
限額，但額度下達到資金撥付往往需要數月時間，預期對於第四季的經濟影響有
限，使今年全年經濟成長率預估將落在 4.5%附近。此外，受歐盟右派勢力崛起及
保護主義影響，歐盟對中國貿易政策可能持續趨嚴，後續仍須關注雙方談判及溝
通結果，未來若外需動能減弱，預期中國政府將持續透過發展特定高科技產業及
扶植內需來支持經濟成長動能，以減輕房市不振及貿易環境惡化的衝擊。

五、印度及東南亞—新、馬出口保持強勁增速，菲律賓央行大舉升息 
(1)印度: 10 月 HSBC 綜合採購經理人指數（PMI）初值上升 0.8 點至 56.7，其中，
製造業 PMI 略下滑 0.2 點至 54.9、服務業 PMI 則上揚 1.0 點至 56.2，皆保持穩健
擴張態勢。由於美國、英國、中東及孟加拉等地的訂單增加，帶動新訂單及出口
訂單加速成長，製造商採購庫存增速創八個月來最快、成品庫存增速亦創歷史新
高，然而聘僱指數降至今年以來最低、未完成訂單下降，反映現階段人力充足。
另一方面，投入成本漲幅連續第七個月下降並降至近一年低點，產品售價漲幅亦
明顯放緩，通膨壓力緩解，有助提升企業獲利能力及消費者購買力，並且給予印
度央行（RBI）貨幣政策空間。 
(2)新加坡：9 月非石油國內出口（NODX）年增 68.4%，增速史上最快，連續第
23 個月正成長，其中，人工智慧需求強勁，帶動電子產品出口躍增 107.6%；非電
子產品出口亦大幅成長 54.2%，主要由船舶和遊艇組件所帶動。按出口目的地劃
分 ， 對香港出口激增 121.1%、 對台灣 、 韓國出口增幅達96%， 對美出口成長 73.2%，
但對印度出口下降 5.6%。展望未來，新加坡金融管理局在 10 月的宏觀經濟評估
報告中表示，直至 2027 年人工智慧 (AI) 驅動的成長將保持相對穩定， 支持未
來幾季經濟成長表現。由於基數較高以及人工智慧相關投資預計放緩，全球利率
上升可能會對新加坡主要貿易夥伴的消費和非人工智慧投資造成壓力，可能會影
響外部需求，薪資成長放緩、招聘謹慎以及運輸成本上升可能會在 2026 年下半
年對可自由支配支出和零售活動構成壓力，預計 2027 年 GDP 成長將放緩。 
(3)印尼：印尼央行 （BI）10 月會議連續第四次會議維持基準利率(7 天期逆回購利
率,BI-Rate)於 5.75%不變，貸款便利利率（LFR）維持在 6.50%不變，但將隔夜存
款便利利率（DFR）調高 25 個基點至 5%，旨在提高印尼盾資產的回報率，藉此
吸引並留住外資，同時宣布分兩階段調降存款準備率，11 月 1 日起下調 1 個百分
點至 8%，12 月 1 日起進一步降至 7%，以提供銀行體系流動性。整體而言，印尼
央行爲捍衛印尼盾，在 5 至 6 月間共升息 100 個基點，同時調降外資避險成本，
並採取「資金停留愈久、優惠愈高」的機制吸引外資流入。惟近期國際指數編纂
公司 MSCI 擬將印尼股市由”新興市場”降級為”獨立市場”，引發資金外流疑慮，
央行選擇利率按兵不動，搭配降準提供銀行體系流動性，以兼顧印尼盾穩定與國
內經濟需求，未來 MSCI 檢討進展(最快 2027 年 4 月公布)及印尼盾走勢，將持續
牽動央行後續政策。

(4)菲律賓：菲律賓央行（BSP）10 月 22 日宣布大幅調升基準利率(逆回購利率)2
碼至 5.50%，為 2023 年以來最大幅度升息 。 央行指出 ， 9 月通膨率已升至 7.2%，
遠高於 2%-4%的目標區間；同時核心通膨持續攀升，顯示價格上漲已由食品與能
源擴散至整體經濟。BSP 亦擔憂中東情勢推升國際油價、運輸費率調漲，以及聖
嬰現象衝擊農業生產，進一步推高物價。此外，披索貶至歷史低點附近，增加進
口通膨風險，因此採取較激進的緊縮措施以穩定市場預期，貨幣政策重點已由支
持經濟成長轉向優先遏制通膨風險。 
(5)馬來西亞：9 月出口額年增 54.5%至 MYR2,151 億元，其中電子&電氣產品出
口大增 69.2%，持續反映 AI 帶動的強勁需求，礦產品出口亦大幅成長 56.3%，抵
銷農產品出口續跌 5.7%的影響，對台灣、美國出口大增 175.2%、89.9%、對新加
坡出口增幅亦達 50.4%。9 月進口額成長 52.7%至 MYR1,814 億元，受惠出口引
申需求，中間產品、資本貨物進口分別成長69.6%、20.9%，消費品進口轉為正成
長 18.2%，自中國和新加坡進口分別成長 67.6%、90.7%。進出口相抵，9 月貿易
順差達 MYR337 億元、年增 64.9%。 
六、台灣—外銷訂單創新高，產業景氣冷熱不均 
9 月外銷訂單達 1,142.1 億美元，再創單月新高，年增 62.4%，主要受惠於人
工智慧 、 高效能運算與雲端服務等應用蓬勃發展 ，以及消費性電子新品陸續推出 ，
帶動相關供應鏈維持高檔；加上國際原物料價格上漲，推升整體接單表現。惟各
產業接單動能仍呈分化，科技產品需求強勁，傳統產業景氣則相對疲弱。經濟部
指出，雖有年底節慶補貨潮挹注，但傳統產業部分產能過剩，終端需求仍未見明
顯回升。

US Trends

2026 年10 月 22 日

1/5 
2026 年 10 月 FOMC プレビュー 
～インフレ認識と AI 投資の持続性～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  前田 和馬 
（℡：050-5473-3888）

（要旨） 
○ 10 月 FOMC（10/27～28 開催）では政策金利が 3.75～4.00％に据え置かれる可能性が高い。
FRB は 9 月に約 3 年振りとなる利上げに踏み切った一方、追加の政策調整を急がず、今後の
経済指標を見極める姿勢を示すとみられる。 
○ ウォーシュ議長の記者会見では、予想を上振れるインフレ動向に関する評価、及び一部のAI
クラウド業者による信用不安が今後の設備投資に与える影響を巡って、何らかの見解が示さ
れるかが注目される。もっとも、市場で織り込まれる12 月 FOMCの利上げを巡っては、具体
的な言及を避ける可能性が高い。 
○ FRBは5つのタスクフォースのうちの生産性と雇用（Productivity and Jobs）部会におい
て、11月16 日にサンフランシスコで公聴会を開催する予定だ。年末までに取りまとめられ
る提言や議論の進捗に関して、ウォーシュ議長から何らかの言及があるかが注目される。

### Page 2

2/5 
・据え置きが見込まれる 10 月FOMC 
2026 年10月FOMC（10/27～28開催）において、FRBは賛成多数で政策金利（3.75～4.00%）を据
え置く可能性が高い。FRBは 9 月FOMCにて米国経済の堅調な推移やインフレ高止まりを踏まえ、23
年 7 月以来となる0.25%の利上げに踏み切った。また、同時に公表されたドットチャートで年内に
追加で 1回の利上げ見通しが示された。8 月CPI及びFRBのタカ派的なスタンスを踏まえて、金融市
場は連続利上げの可能性を徐々に織り込んでいったものの、FRB高官は9月末から10 月初めにか
けてこれに慎重なスタンスを示した。例えば、9/29にウィリアムズ総裁は「急ぐ必要はなく、情
報を集める時間がある」 、10/1 にジェファーソン副議長は「 （政策判断には）もう少し時間がかか
るかもしれない」とそれぞれ述べた。 
この結果、本稿執筆時点におけるFF金利先物市場（Fed Watch）において、83.9%の確率で金利
据え置きが見込まれる一方、0.25%ptの利上げ予想は16.1%に留まる（資料1）。なお、金融市場は
12 月FOMCにおける0.25%の利上げに加えて、2027年末に向けて更に 2回程度の利上げを織り込ん
でいる（資料 2）。この場合の合計利上げ幅は+1.0%ptとなり、9月時点のドットチャート（26年に
計 0.5%ptの利上げを行い、27 年は据え置き）を上回る。もっとも、過去の利上げ局面ではそれが
1～2 回に留まることは稀であり、金融市場はこうした過去の事例も一部に織り込んでいるとみら
れる（詳細は 2026年 9月 18日付け「ＦＲＢの過去の利上げサイクルと長期金利の行方」）。

3/5 
slight growth） 」とまとめられた。また、 「労働市場は総じて変化がなかった」と指摘した。一
方、「物価上昇は3 地区で力強く上昇し、8 地区で緩やかに上昇した。8地区のうち3地区では投
入価格の上昇圧力が強まっている」とインフレ高止まりへの懸念が強調された。また、複数の地
区でインフレによる生活費負担の増大が賃金の上昇圧力になっている可能性が指摘された。 
10 月FOMCまでには10/27に 9 月耐久財受注や10月コンファレンスボード消費者信頼感指数など
が公表されるものの、10月FOMCの政策判断に大きな影響を与える可能性は低い。

・インフレ認識、AI投資の持続性、タスクフォース 
9 月FOMCの声明文では「経済活動は堅調に拡大している」との景気認識が維持された。一方、 「イ
ンフレ率は高止まりしている（7 月時点：インフレ率は2%目標に対して高止まりしている。これは
エネルギーを含む一部セクターにおける供給ショックを通じた価格上昇を反映している） 」と、供
給ショックに関する言及が前回から削除された。10 月FOMCにおいても、こうした景気・物価認識は
概ね維持される可能性が高い。一方、物価認識に関して、足下のインフレ減速の鈍化や加速の兆し
を指摘する、或いはインフレ高止まりの背景として需要の強さに言及する場合、タカ派的なスタン
スと捉えられるだろう。 
ウォーシュ議長は記者会見において、次回 12 月FOMCに関する具体的な言及を避けるとみられる
一方、下記3 点に関して新たな見解が示されるかが焦点となろう。 
まず、足下のインフレ認識に変化があるかだ。9 月FOMCの記者会見で議長は「インフレ率は高止
まりしており、その状態はあまりに長く続いている」や「あまりに多くの品目が 3％を超えるイン
フレ率にある」と述べた。また、同会合の議事要旨では何名か（some）の参加者が「AI普及を通じ
て、需要が中期的に供給を上回り、インフレ上昇への圧力となる可能性がある」と指摘した。その
後公表された 9 月のコアCPIは予想を上振れるなど、高関税による財価格の押し上げやエネルギー
高による輸送サービスへの波及の兆しがみられる。ウォーシュ議長がこうした需要と供給双方のイ
ンフレ要因に関して、新たな見解を示すのかが注目される。 
次に、AI投資の持続性への見方だ。FOMC後の10/29に公表される7～9月期実質GDP成長率は前期
から加速すると見込まれ、AI関連を中心とした設備投資の拡大、及び資産効果（株高）による消費
の堅調さが示されるとみられる。ウォーシュ議長は 10/16 の講演において「世界経済は 5～6 か月
前に予測できなかったほどの回復力を示している」と述べ、エネルギー高や利上げに対する経済的
な耐性を強調した。一方、足下ではAIモデル開発業者などに計算資源を提供する「ネオクラウド企
業」の一部で信用不安が浮上しており、データセンターを中心としたAI関連投資が減速するとの懸
念が強まっている。ウォーシュ議長がAI投資の先行き、及び一部企業の信用不安が金融市場全体に
与える影響に関して、どのような見解を示すのかが焦点となる。 
最後に、タスクフォースの進捗状況が部分的にでも示され、2027年以降の政策運営に関するヒン
トがみられるかだ。ウォーシュ議長は就任後の6月に 5 つのタスクフォース（①コミュニケーショ
ン、②バランスシート政策、③既存データの活用、④変化する時代の生産性と雇用、⑤FRBのインフ
レ枠組み）の設置を発表し、FRB改革を推進する姿勢を示した。5つのタスクフォースは秋までに何

### Page 4

4/5 
らかの成果を得たうえで、2026 年末までにFRBへ提言をまとめる予定だ。ウォーシュ議長は 10/16
の講演にて、11 月 16 日にサンフランシスコで生産性と雇用に関する公聴会を開催すると述べてお
り、これを含むタスクフォースの進捗状況が開示されるか注目される。

据え置くべきとの考えを示した

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告

10/12
クリーブランド連銀
ハマック総裁*
インフレ持続の背景として、企業投資の急拡大や資産効果を通じた
消費押し上げによる需要増を指摘した。こうした需要をある程度減
速させる必要性を強調したほか、投資需要を抑制することで雇用を
悪化させずにインフレを抑制できる可能性を指摘した
10/13
ニューヨーク連銀
ウィリアムズ総裁*
年内に追加で1回の利上げを支持した一方、2027年に関しては追加利
上げの必要はないと明言した。この背景として、来年はエネルギー
価格がインフレを押し上げないこと、及び関税の影響が一服するこ
とのほか、足下の米国経済が全体として過熱していないことを指摘
した
10/14
サンフランシスコ連銀
デイリー総裁

「（利上げが）必要となる可能性が高いが、必要な時間はある」と
述べるなど、10月FOMCの利上げに慎重な姿勢を示した。また、予想
を上振れた9月CPIを巡って、 「インフレリスクは依然として高いが、
リスクは現実に起きたわけではない」と、基調的なインフレ動向に
大きな変化が起きていないとの認識を示唆した
10/16 ウォーシュ議長*
AI関連を中心に「投資の急増」が生じていると指摘し、世界経済が
エネルギーショックに対する想定以上の強靭性を示しているとの楽
観的な見方に言及した。一方、先行きの政策決定には「予断を持た
ない」と述べるなど、10月FOMCに関するヒントは与えなかった
注：*は 2026 年に投票権を持つFOMC メンバー。

2026 年 10 月 30 日

調査レポート

2026 年 7～9 月期の GDP（1 次速報）予測

～ほぼゼロ成長も、緩やかな景気回復は継続～

調査部 主任研究員 藤田 隼平

2026 年 11 月 16 日に内閣府が公表する 7～9 月期の実質 GDP（1 次速報値）は、前期比＋0.0%（前期比年
率換算＋0.1%）と予測する。設備投資や政府消費の増加に支えられ、かろうじて 4 四半期連続のプラス成長を
維持したものの、個人消費や輸出が前期の一時的な押し上げ要因の剥落もあって減少したことで、ほぼゼロ成長
にとどまったと見込まれる。なお、輸入が鉱物性燃料を中心に持ち直したことで外需寄与度は大きめのマイナスと
なるものの、公的在庫のプラス寄与により、おおむね相殺されたと考えられる。 
また、名目 GDP は、前期比＋0.9%（年率換算＋3.8%）となったと見込まれる。コスト増加分の価格転嫁が進
む中で、GDP デフレーターは前年比＋2.7%（季節調整済前期比では＋0.9%）と高めの伸びが続いた。 
今回、7～9 月期の実質 GDP はほぼゼロ成長にとどまる見込みだが、イラン情勢の悪化という逆風を踏まえれ
ば、国内景気は底堅さを維持している 。先行きについては、輸入コストの高止まりに伴う価格転嫁の進展が景気
の重しとなる可能性はあるものの、良好な雇用・所得環境や企業業績の改善を背景に内需の回復基調が維持さ
れることなどから、国内景気は緩やかな回復を続けると期待される。

（注）内需寄与度、民間在庫、外需寄与度は実質 GDP に対する寄与度 
（出所）内閣府「四半期別 GDP 速報」により作成

### Page 2

2 / 2

需要項目別の動向は以下の通りである。

① 実質個人消費（前期比 －0.1%） 
 良好な所得環境が維持され る中、消費者マインドも持ち直しへ向かったものの、前期に 耐久財
が自動車やエアコンを中心に 一時的な要因によって増加した反動 が生じた とみられる 。価格転
嫁の進展による 物価上昇 、地震や台風といった自然災害の発生 も重しとなった 可能性がある 。 
② 実質住宅投資（前期比 －0.3%） 
 住宅投資に先行する住宅着工戸数 が横ばいとなる中 、建築コストが上昇していることもあり、 住
宅投資は小幅ながら減少が続いたとみられる 。 
③ 実質設備投資（前期比 ＋0.9%） 
 企業の投資意欲は 省力化やデジタル需要 などに支えられて 堅調であり 、設備投資は 3 四半期
ぶりに増加 したと見込まれる 。機械設備投資の供給側統計である資本財総供給（除く輸送機械）
も 7～9 月期は前期比 ＋5.3%と増加した。 
④ 実質民間在庫（前期比寄与度 －0.0%ポイント ） 
 前期に 供給制約懸念の高まりを受けて、メーカーが製品在庫などの積み増しを進めた 反動もあ
り、7～9 月期はマイナス 寄与になったと考えられる 。 
 なお、公的在庫 について は、政府による代替調達の進展により 石油備蓄の 減少ペースが大きく鈍
化したことから、前期比寄与度 ＋0.5% ポイントと プラス寄与になったと みられる 。 
⑤ 実質政府最終消費支出（前期比＋ 0.3%） 
 高齢化が進む中、医療費 ・介護費を中心に 底堅く推移 したと予測する。  
⑥ 実質公共投資（前期比 －2.7%） 
 建築・土木コストの上昇が続いている中、 7～8 月の建設総合統計（出来高ベース：公共） は弱
めの動きとなった ことから 、実質公共投資は 2 四半期 連続で 減少したと見込まれる。  
⑦ 外需（前期比寄与度 －0.5%ポイント 、実質輸出：前期比 －0.5%、実質輸入：同 ＋1.7%） 
 輸出は 前期比 －0.5%と減少したと予測する。財は 堅調な 米国向け や中東向けの持ち直しを受
けて増加 した一方、サービスは 前期に 研究開発サービスが一時 的な 要因により上振れた 反動
で減少 したとみられる。  
 輸入 は前期比 ＋1.7%と増加したと 予測する。 特に財が鉱物性燃料 を中心に 持ち直した。ただ
し、サービス については 減少した 可能性がある 。 
 結果 、輸出から輸入を差し引いた 外需 の寄与度は －0.5%ポイント とマイナス になった と見込ま
れる。

營收走勢圖

研究員/吳大任 TERRY.WU@uni-psg.com

研究部對聯發科的投資建議維持強力買進 ， 目標價5980 元 ， 係因
(1)3Q26 財報大幅優於預期，(2)AI 加速貢獻幅度上修，4Q26 財
測顯著優於市場預期，(3)再度上修 AI 加速器貢獻營收幅度，也
上修市占率，且已有第二客戶，另外 448G SERDES 產品已經 TAPE 
OUT，(4)營收規模擴張速度快 ， 但費用控管好 ，OPM 將開始出現規
模效應，27 年 OPM 以 20%以上為目標。綜合以上，上修聯發科26
年 EPS 預估為 73.12 元(原估 66 元，+11.3%YOY)，並上修 27 年
EPS 預估為 141.16 元(原估 121.05 元，+93.1%YOY)，也上修 28
年 EPS 預估為 298.72 元(原估256.7 元，+112%YOY)。 因為聯發科
的 AI 營收占比推估在 27 年將超過手機，28 年將會成為最大的營
收來源，故以 27 年的 40XPER 或是 28 年的 20XPER，目標價上修
至 5980 元，因為近期盤勢大幅回檔，潛在上漲空間較大，故維持
強力買進評等。

重點摘要： 
1. 3Q26 財報大幅優於預期： 
聯發科 3Q26 營收為 1681 億元，成長 10.4%QOQ，成長
18.3%YOY，其中主要是 SMART EDGE 成長 20%QOQ 所致(財
測僅給出高個位數增長)，其餘手機及 PMIC 事業體表現約
略符合預期，在法說會中公司表示除了需求強勁外，供應
鏈改善推動也是原因之一。毛利率 46.3%，相對於上一季
提升 0.1 個百分點 ， 與原預估相當 。 雖然營收大規模成長 ，
不過費用控管得宜 ， 使得OPM 攀升至 16.1%(原估 15%)，稅
後淨利達到 282 億元(原估 252.9 億元)，成長 14.6%QOQ，
成長 10.6%YOY，EPS 17.37 元(原估 15.77 元)。

2. 4Q26 AI ASIC 出貨規模上修，超越市場預期： 
公司在法說會表示 ， 雖然3Q26 營收基期較高 ， 加上消費性
電子需求疲軟 ， 不過在AI 加速器量產帶動下 ， 且上修4Q26
貢獻營收程度至 25 億美元(原估 20 億美元)，營收財測給
出 2101 億元至 2218 億元之間 ，1 美元兌 31.7 元新台幣做
計算，成長 25.-32%QOQ，成長 40-48%YOY，毛利率預測為 
45%（±1.5 個百分點） ，營業費用率預測為 26%（±2 個百
分點） ， 並且宣告4Q26 將成為聯發科 OPM 向上轉捩點的開
端。綜合以上，研究部上修 4Q26 營收預估為 2207.3 億元
(原估 2139 億元)，成長 31.3%QOQ，成長 47%YOY，毛利率
上修預估為 44.9%(原估 41.5%)，OPM 同步上修預估為
19.4%(原估 15%)，稅後淨利同步上修為 4090 億元(原估
3208 億元)，成長 46.7%QOQ，成長 78.4%YOY，EPS 25.48
元(原估 EPS 20 元)。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

3. AI 加速器部分只有不斷的好消息，跟市場傳聞相差甚遠： 
A.雲端 CSP 客戶 
聯發科此次法說會上修 AI ASIC 26/27 年營收貢獻，26 年
從 20 億美元上修至 25 億美元，27 年貢獻營收規模達到
160 億美元(原估 120 億美元)，市占率從先前的16-18%上
修至 20%。上修係因聯發科在供應鏈包含材料的供應商協
調順利所致。至於市場更關心的 CSP 客戶下一代加速器，
雖然目前仍碰到良率及載板問題，且公司此次也揭露將會
1Q28 進行量產，因為是CHIPLET 架構，故其中幾顆DIE 已
經完成 TAPE OUT，但較複雜的DIE 將需要較多時間，不過
也按照計畫進行。另外在先進封裝部分，積極與 IDM 及載
板廠商合作，包含日本及台灣供應商，確實有碰到一些困
難，不過距離量產時間還有超過一年，故公司表示仍有信
心準時量產。除此之外，公司也在下一代晶片尋求更多的
產能 ， 且不依賴單一供應商 。 故研究部先前預估28 年聯發
科 AI 加速器貢獻營收達到 320 億美元，研究部目前先暫
時預估不動，未來確認到良率確定上行順利後再進行調
整。 
B.第二個客戶 
公司在法說會上透露， 目前已經有第二個AI 加速器客戶 ，
且與市場上傳聞的中國客戶並非正確，該客戶為非中國廠
商，除此之外，該客戶的產品設計端已經開始，且將在28
年量產 。 研究部認為該客戶為美系客戶 ， 但細節尚未確定 ，
故目前仍未預估入模型當中。 
C.448G SERDES 
目前已經完成 TAPE OUT，持續追趕美系晶片大廠的技術，
雖然在下一代晶片聯發科是提供 336G， 但有望在下下一代
晶片趕上。

4. 獲利將有結構性的提升： 
雖然 AI 加速器的產品毛利率低於公司平均，但因為加速
器單價較高，使營收規模快速放大，而費用可以控制在一
個相對緩步成長的水準，故聯發科認為 4Q26 將會是公司
OPM 向上拐點的開端，27 年的平均 OPM 將會提升至 20%以
上。過去聯發科僅有在疫情時代，產品急速漲價那時候才
有達到 OPM 20%以上的狀況，但後來因為 WFH 結束，庫存
高度堆積，跌損的反噬，使得獲利率大幅回落。本次在AI 
ASIC 帶動，OPM 重回擴張階段，在 AI ASIC 案件有延續性
的前提下，推估聯發科 27-28 年 OPM 將會持續朝 MID 20%
邁進。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

5. 獲利率優化，持續上修： 
聯發科3Q26受惠SMART EDGE產品需求強勁，加上供應鏈協
調順利，使得財報優於預期，另外在4Q26方面，又上修AI
加速器的貢獻預估，且獲利結構也比研究部預估的更樂
觀，故研究部上修26年聯發科營收預估為6901.2億元(原
估6690億元)，成長15.8%YOY，稅後淨利同步上修預估為
1173億元(原估1059億元)，成長11.3%YOY，EPS 73.07 元
(原估66元)，而27年雖然研究部仍有下修手機及 SMART 
EDGE產線 ， 不過因為AI加速器將貢獻160億美元 ， 故上修27
年 聯 發 科 營 收 預 估1.18 兆元 ( 原估 1.13 兆元 ) ， 成 長
71%YOY，稅後淨利同步上修預估為 2264億元(原估1942億
元)，成長93.1%YOY，EPS 141.06元(原估EPS 121元)。而
28年在第九代產品在 1Q28開始貢獻後，因為該產品使用
2nm製程 ， 加上設計更為複雜 ， 聯發科將參與IP及架構等設
計，單價會大幅提升，加上聯發科OPM結構將持續優化下，
28年聯發科營收預估為 2.21兆元(原估2.17兆元)，成長
87.5%YOY，稅後淨利上修預估為 4794.4億元(原估4117億
元)，成長111.8%YOY，EPS 298.72元(原估256.7元)。

### Page 1

Summary: We attended the 2026 Taiwan Printed Circuit Association (TPCA) Show that 
was held on 20-22 October. Below, we share our read-throughs from booth visits and key 
company takeaways. Overall, we remain positive on Taiwan glass fibre, CCL, PCB, and 
substrate vendors, supported by upbeat company commentary. On pricing, we expect 
low-end CCL and E-glass price increases to be larger and more sustained than previously 
anticipated. High-end CCL vendor commentary suggests plans for more meaningful price 
increases from 2H26 to maintain their products’ relative profitability amid sharp increases 
in low-end CCL prices. We also expect prices for T-glass, ABF, CCL, and substrates to 
continue rising. On the CCL technology roadmap, we favour Q-glass combined with 
hydrocarbon resin (HC) over PTFE-based alternatives as the longer-term direction. Lastly, 
we provide our view on the CCL market competition landscape change after seeing 
Nan Ya Plastics targeting the high-end market more aggressively. We also provide some of 
our key highlights from related companies within the Taiwan PCB supply chain. EMC (2383 
TT, TWD4,750, Buy [1]), ITEQ (6213 TT, TWD746, Buy [1]), Kinsus (3189 TT, TWD941, 
Buy [1]) and NYPCB (8046 TT, TWD1,255, Buy [1]) are our top ideas within our coverage.

We attended the 2026 Taiwan Printed Circuit Association (TPCA) Show last week. Below, 
we share our read-throughs from booth visits and key company takeaways.

Whether to adopt PTFE emerged as a key point of debate at the show.  
• During the TPCA show, Taiwan Union Technology (TUC; 6274 TT, TWD1,280, Buy [1]) 
unveiled its new M10 (Hydrocarbon base) and M11 (PTFE base) materials with glass-
fabric (Low-Dk2, Q-glass) and glass-fabric-free versions. Nan Ya Plastic (1303 TT, not 
rated) also unveiled its M11-grade material (ie, NPG-A99) at the show. Based on our 
understanding, NPG-A99 will continue to a hydrocarbon resin (HC) base, likely 
combined with Low-Dk2, to enhance CCL performance for high frequency and high-
speed signal transmission requirements. However, Elite Material (EMC; 2383 TT, 
TWD4,750, Buy [1]) remains reluctant to enter the PTFE-based CCL market, citing 
limited end application. Also, EMC plans to use advanced Q-glass for its M10 CCL. 
Lastly, we also heard from Panasonic’s booth representative that Panasonic (6752 JP, 
JPY3,687, Neutral [3]; covered by Ayaka Inomata) is pursuing a similar strategy for 
M9/M10 development, leveraging Q-glass instead of PTFE at this stage.

• While Nvidia (NVDA US, USD216, Outperform [2]; covered by Louis Miscioscia) 
appears keen to enhance product performance by adopting PTFE, given its very low 
dissipation factor (Df), PCB manufacturing yields remain a key concern. Accordingly, 
some leading vendors are broadening their material options (including PTFE-based 
CCL) to address potential customer requirements. EMC and Panasonic, however, 
remain firmly committed to a Q-glass-based technology roadmap. Although TUC 
highlighted progress on its PTFE-based M11 at a technology conference during the 
show, we believe it also intends to advance its existing HC resin/Low-Dk2 and HC 
resin/Q-glass roadmap for its next-generation CCL. In our view, its PTFE-based M11 
development is intended to preserve flexibility across potential technology pathways 
rather than replace its existing approach. Nan Ya Plastics’s strategy also appears 
reasonable: it can produce Low-Dk glass in-house but must source Q-glass and PTFE 
externally. This sourcing structure may explain its continued use of Low-Dk2 with HC-
resin instead of PTFE for its next-gen products, including M11.

 Taiwan PCB: Our key takeaways from 2026 TPCA show

### Page 2

Taiwan Data Centre Hardware 
27 October 2026 
2

• Conclusion: which technology roadmap will prevail? We believe it is too early to 
identify a clear winner, as the supply chain continues to improve manufacturing yields 
for PTFE-based products. Some vendors remain cautious despite PTFE’s long history in 
the industry, as its thermal shrinkage and relative softness still make adoption in high-
layer-count (HLC) PCBs challenging. Given the current stance of leading global vendors 
EMC, Doosan (000150 KS), and Panasonic, together with EMC’s execution track 
record, we currently favour Q-glass-based solutions without PTFE as the more likely 
direction. Nevertheless, broadening product offerings to address different customer 
requirements should not be viewed negatively, provided vendors have sufficient 
resources to support development across multiple technology pathways.

Low-end CCL and e-glass continue to surprise on the upside.  
• During the show, we also spoke with Kingboard Laminates (1888 HK, not rated). The 
company remained positive on the E-glass outlook, although its booth representative 
provided no numerical guidance for next year following a c.200% price hike in 2026. 
Kingboard attributed the pricing strength to supply constraints, driven largely by AI 
server demand. While we highlighted increased glass yarn supply from Chinese peers in 
2Q/3Q26, the company argued that the weaving machine capacity remains the 
bottleneck. It expects to add only c.500 weaving machines in 2026 and 1.2k in 2027 (vs. 
current installed base of 3.3k). E-glass fabric price increases therefore appear 
supported by limited weaving capacity and producers shifting capacity from lower-end 
glass to higher-end products, including Low-Dk, Low-Dk2, and T-glass. Kingboard also 
noted a similar trend in low-end copper foil, where shortages have recently intensified.

• The read-through from Nan Ya Plastics is similar. The company indicated that its current 
fibreglass allocation between internal consumption and external sales is 45:55, with the 
internal share set to rise gradually as it upgrades its product mix. Its CCL portfolio 
remains concentrated in FR-4 (M2 grade), but it plans to migrate toward M4/M6, 
supported by long-term contract wins with two major hyperscalers seeking to secure 
CCL supply. In 2027, M4/M6 products are expected to account for 60-70% of its mix (4m 
out of 6.5m in capacity), with M2 and below contributing 20%. The remainder would 
comprise high-end CCL, including M7/M8/M9, reflecting recent development progress. 
We therefore expect Nan Ya’s external thin-type E-glass supply to decline as its M4/M6 
mix increases next year.

Competition from Nan Ya Plastics.   
• The current CCL shortage creates favourable conditions for Nan Ya Plastics to expand 
into the high-end CCL supply chain. Whether the company has entered AMZN’s T3/T4 
supply chain is also a key point of market debate. Based on discussions with its booth 
representatives, however, we believe management will prioritise upgrading its M4/M6 
product portfolio before pursuing the highest-end CCL market more aggressively. On the 
positive side, favourable supply-demand dynamics have supported Nan Ya’s transition 
from a low-end supplier toward the mid-to-high-end segment, although high-end CCL 
should still contribute a relatively small share of revenue in 2027. For incumbent 
suppliers, competition is clearly intensifying, but we expect the near-term impact on 
high-end peers to remain limited.

• Our read: We expect the impact on EMC and TUC to remain relatively limited in 2027. 
Competitive pressure could become more meaningful over 2028-2030, although it is too 
early to assess its magnitude.

### Page 3

Taiwan Data Centre Hardware 
27 October 2026 
3

Other key takeaways: 
• Nitto Boseki (3110 JP, JPY2,942, Neutral [3]; covered by Noritsugu Hirakawa) identified 
Taiwan Glass (1802 TT, NR) and Grace Fabric Technology (603256 CH, CNY84.95, 
Outperform [2]; covered by Dennis Ip) as key competitors. Its plan to triple capacity by 
2028 remains largely on track. The company also intends to expand capacity further by 
2030, although the details have yet to be confirmed.

• Resonac (4004 JP, JPY12,915, Outperform [2]; covered by Hidemitsu Umebayashi) 
indicated that customers are requesting ABF substrate core thicknesses of 2,000-
3,000μm, versus its current maximum of 1,600μm, to accommodate larger package 
sizes. Potential technology pathways include adopting glass cores or ceramic 
substrates, or stacking additional core layers to enhance rigidity. Based on discussions 
with booth representatives, substrate makers may move toward multi-core structures, 
which could increase T-glass consumption. However, technical challenges in enabling 
signal transmission between core layers remain a potential barrier that would need to be 
addressed over the next one to two years.

• Mitsui Kinzoku (5706 JP, JPY2,054, Outperform [2]; covered by Shinichiro Ozaki) 
indicated that, based on its technology roadmap, M10-grade CCL combined with its 
HVLP5 copper foil should support 448Gbps SerDes transmission. In the near term, the 
company plans to shift capacity from lower-end to higher-end HVLP products, with a key 
piece of production equipment scheduled for installation in 2028/29. On pricing, its booth 
representative suggested that quarterly increases of more than 10% would be 
appropriate. Regarding copper foil shortages highlighted by other supply-chain 
participants, the representative indicated that mid-tier products, such as HVLP1 and 
HVLP2, could face the most severe supply constraints.

• Unimicron (3037 TT, TWD1,060, Buy [1]) appeared to highlight multi-core structures and 
the embedding of passive components within core layers as key technology trends in 
ABF substrates.

• Zhen Ding Technology (ZDT; 4958 TT, not rated) indicated that its substrate business 
has a plan to list on the Hong Kong Stock Exchange in 1H27 under the name Leading 
Interconnect Semiconductor Technology (LIST). At the show, the company focused 
primarily on thick HDI boards and highlighted two next-generation technology trends: 1) 
sequential lamination (M+N) to improve signal integrity and increase routing density 
within the same footprint; and 2) hybrid HDI/HLC structures, which add HDI layers to 
thick HLC boards (M+N), as discussed earlier.

### Page 1

M
Idea
3Q GM likely to decline on higher costs and slower new product adoption…
Lotes reported unaudited 3Q26 revenue of NT$9,924M (+4% q/q, +18% y/y). Higher
copper, gold and plastics costs for legacy products, coupled with slower new
product adoption, should continue to pressure GM. We forecast 3Q GM of 44.6% (-
90bps q/q), below consensus of 45.8% (+30bps q/q). However, a decline in opex to
NT$2.0B, consistent with management commentary, should support operating

profit growth of 17% q/q to NT$2,452M, albeit still 6% below consensus. We
forecast NP of NT$2,174M (+10% q/q) and EPS of NT$19.36, 4% below the Street.
…but we see 3Q as the GM trough, with recovery beginning in 4Q: Following
slower  server platform transitions, we expect adoption to accelerate from 4Q26.
Socket production for AMD's Venice CPU will ramp from 4Q26 and socket
production for Intel’s Oak Stream CPU will ramp in 1Q27. These transitions should

enable Lotes to reprice products and pass through higher input costs, supporting
margin recovery. Our industry checks also point to a rebound in ODM server board
production in 4Q, following a modest 3Q decline. We forecast 4Q revenue of ~NT
$10.5B (+5% q/q), GM of 45.9% (+130bps q/q) and EPS of NT$21.11 (+9% q/q).
2027 - margin recovery and new AI product ramps should drive earnings growth:
Rising adoption of new CPU platforms should support socket margin recovery,

alongside growing contributions from QD, SOCAMM and NPO. QD reached 2–3% of
revenue in September, with further growth expected in 4Q26 and 2027, and we
forecast SOCAMM will reach ~7% of revenue in CY27. Lotes has two NPO
opportunities, one through LINTES and another directly, with the direct program
expected to ramp in 2H27. Although initial contributions should remain modest, we
see NPO as a longer-term AI data center growth opportunity and a potential re-

rating catalyst. Overall, we see the AI revenue mix, including QD, SOCAMM and
NPO, rising from ~5% in CY25 to slightly above 20% by CY28.
Upgrade to OW and raise PT by 15% to NT$2,350, driven by our estimate increases
for CY27-28. Our new PT implies 22x 2027e P/E and 16.5x 2028e P/E, above the past
3-year average of 17.4x, which we view as reasonable amid a rising AI revenue mix.

3Q26 Results Preview
3Q26 preview:  We forecast revenue of NT$9.92B (+4% q/q, +18% y/y), broadly in line
with the Street. Although gross margin should decline 80bps q/q to 44.6%, a 12%
sequential reduction in operating expenses should lift operating margin 270bps q/q to
24.7%. Nevertheless, this remains 130bps below consensus, implying operating profit of
NT$2.45bn, 6% below the Street. We therefore see potential for an earnings miss, as we

estimate net profit of ~NT$2,174M (+10% q/q, -6% y/y), for EPS of NT$19.36, 4% below
the Street.

### Page 6

6
Price Target Discussion and Valuation Methodology
Our price target increases ~15%, to NT$2,350 from NT$2,045, driven mainly by our
higher earnings estimates for 2027-28.
Our new PT implies ~22x 2027e P/E, slightly above its three-year average forward P/E of
17.4x, but still below that of peer Amphenol (25-35x 2027e P/E, based on Visible Alpha
consensus). We think Lotes stock could  re-rate to a higher valuation multiple if margins
hold up well and Lotes' exposure to AI PCs and AI servers grows in the coming years.

We continue to derive our PT, which is our base case scenario value, from a residual
income model (RIM), consistent with the methodology we apply to the rest of our tech
hardware coverage universe. To calculate the intrinsic value of the stock, we use the
company's beginning equity plus the present value of all expected residual income of
earnings in excess of the cost of capital. The RI is positive when ROAE is above the cost of
capital and negative when it is below the cost of capital.

Our key RIM assumptions (all unchanged) include a cost of equity of 9.2% (beta of ~1.0,
equity risk premium of ~8.7% and risk-free rate of ~1.0%), a medium-term growth rate of
12.5% and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Our bull and bear case values rise to NT$3,400 and NT1,285, respectively, in line with
the increase in magnitude of our base case scenario.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 7

UMC (2303.TW)

7
UMC
Company description
United Microelectronics Corporation (UMC) is a leading global semiconductor
foundry company. It provides high-quality IC fabrication services, focusing on
logic and various specialty technologies to serve all major sectors of the
electronics industry. UMC's comprehensive IC processing technologies and
manufacturing solutions include Logic/Mixed-Signal, embedded High-
Voltage, embedded Non-Volatile-Memory, RFSOI, and BCD. Most of UMC's

12-inch and 8-inch fabs with core R&D are located in Taiwan.
Investment strategy
We rate UMC shares a Buy. Overall inventory levels in PC, smartphone, and
consumer electronics are currently healthy, and we see limited inventory
correction downside. While macro uncertainties may linger, we believe UMC’s
niche technology offerings will help it outperform its trailing-edge foundry
peers. We also expect transfer orders from TSMC and the AI-on-device trend

to drive stronger demand, and see better UTR recovery for UMC going
forward.
Valuation
Our target price for UMC of NT$125 is based on 18x 2027 /28E EPS, which is
higher than the midpoint of its 3-year forward PE. We believe our target
multiple is justified by our belief that UMC should benefit from the robust
demand in 22/28nm, TSMC's increasing order outflow opportunity, long-term
opportunity in advanced packaging and photonics and that its niche

technology offerings should help it outperform its trailing-edge foundry
peers. Our target price equates to 2027E/28E PB of 3.8x/3.6x.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price include: 1) a longer-than-expected
demand recovery; 2) more competition from peers in 28nm; 3) worse-than-
expected developments from the company's China investment; 4) a
significant decline in smartphone end-demand; 5) poor contribution from
Novatek's iPhone OLED business; and 6) fiercer ASP competition from

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be

period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

M
Update
MediaTek  | Asia Pacific
Positive news on AI ASIC
execution and project win;
reiterate OW
We expect the stock to react positively to the AI ASIC
developments; OW: 1) TPU v9 on schedule, 2) confidence in Intel
EMIB-T substrate yield improvement, and 3) second customer
win. We maintain our  PT of NT$6,188 and Top Pick.
What's new? MediaTek confirms second customer win: In our preview note,  we
had expected some discussion on a potential second customer. Today, management

confirmed the win, and indicated it is not a China CSP customer, hence, we think it
could be a US CSP  customer. We are modeling US$2.5bn revenue from the second
customer in 2028, but management thinks the demand should be higher than that.
Status of major TPU projects - on track: TPU v8 expected to contribute US$2.5bn
revenue in 4Q26 (vs. prior guidance of >US$2bn). Major AI ASIC customer’s TPU v9
is expected to tape out on time and starts mass production in 1Q28, dismissing

recent delay concerns. While TPU v9 volume highly depends on Intel EMIB-T
substrate supply, management has confidence in EMIB-T yield improvement. The
company is also working closely with the customer on the third generation
(TPUv10) and will disclose more details at the appropriate time.
Our key question to management:  Will both major  CSP customers prioritize GPU
or ASIC, if/when we see cloud capex budget shrinkage? Management responded
that, while not every ASIC in the industry can compete with GPU, the ASICs they

help to design (TPUv8 and v9) enjoy very competitive TCO (total cost of
ownership). Thus, we believe MediaTek’s AI ASIC revenue can track our forecast, if
the substrate supply is sufficient.
4Q26 revenue to grow 25-32% Q/Q, with operating margin improvement thanks
to AI ASIC: Gross margin guidance of 45% is slightly better than our 44%-45%,
given some price hikes for smartphone and smart-edge products. After deducting
the US$2.5bn revenue contribution from the AI ASIC in the quarter, the non-AI

business should be down 17% Q/Q.
We think the implied AI ASIC operating margin could be 25%-30% at scale: This
assumes AI ASIC is 35% of 4Q26 revenue and the company improves its 4Q26
operating margin to 19% (up from 16.1% in 3Q26),

Valuation Methodology and Risks
MediaTek (2454.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: cost of equity 9.2%, intermediate growth
rate 12.0%, terminal growth rate 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Edge AI proliferates smartphone replacement cycle.
n Smartphone demand rises in China and other EMs.
n New products attract high demand, resulting in market share gains.
n Google's TPU spurs demand.
Risks to Downside
n Smartphone demand deteriorates in China and other EMs.

n Competition heats up, resulting in pricing competition.
n New products attract low demand, causing market share loss.
n Margin dilution is more severe.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

3-month preview: Landmark’s Sep revenues were up 190% YoY/ 4% MoM to 
NT$540m, supported by strong demand on 800G / 1.6T SiPh optical modules and 
better InP substrate supply. Looking ahead, we expect company’s Oct/ Nov/ Dec 
revenues to see sequential MoM growth at 5%/ 5%/ 8%, resulting in 4Q revenues 
growth at 16% QoQ to NT$1.8bn, with company’s continuous capacity expansion.

### Page 2

Earnings revision: We factor in Landmark 3Q26 result and revise up earnings by 9%/ 
3%/ 2%/ 1% in 2026-29E mainly on higher revenues of SiPh CW lasers, supported by 
strong client demand and capacity expansion. We revise up GM by 1ppts in 2026E on 
mix upgrade, and revise down Opex ratio in 2026-29E to reflect improving operational 
efficiency amid the larger scale.

Valuation: We employ a discounted P/E methodology (unchanged) and apply a 61.5x 
(unchanged, report link) target P/E multiple on our 2029E EPS, discounted back to 
2027E at a COE of 10.5% (beta 1.7x, risk free rate 1.6%, and market risk premium at 
5.1% - unchanged). Our target P/E multiple of 61.5x is derived from the PEG&M ratio of 
peers (unchanged) in the photonics supply chain. Consequently, our 12-month target 
price is now NT$4,490 (vs. previously at NT$4,459). Our new TP implied 2028E P/E of 
75x is between company’s avg. P/E at 62x and avg. +1 P/E at 91x. Maintain Buy.

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - LandMark 
Price Target Risks and Methodology - LandMark Optoelectronics Corp.  
Valuation: We employ a discounted P/E methodology, applying a 61.5x target P/E 
multiple on our 2029E EPS, then discount it back to 2027E at a COE of 10.5% (beta 1.7x, 
risk free rate 1.6% and market risk premium at 5.1%), to derive our 12-month target 
price of NT$4,490. Our target P/E multiple is based on the same PEG&M ratio as peers in 
the photonics supply chain. We are Buy-rated on LandMark. 
Key risk: Slower-than-expected SiPh penetration; stronger-than-expected competition; 
Integrated Device Manufacturers (IDMs) offering Epitaxial (EPI) wafers in-house.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.
Not Rated (NR). The investment rating, target price and earnings estimates (where relevant) are removed pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when
Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that

 결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것 
류형근

hyungkeun.ryu@daishin.com

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 목표주가 500,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.10.29) 
231,000 
반도체업종

◼ 3분기 실적발표에 대한 시장의 반응은 다소 냉소적. 
◼ 주주환원은 시점의 문제일 뿐. 강화 방향성은 여전히 유효. 
◼ 메모리반도체 이익의 폭증이 가격의 매력을 유발. 비중확대 전략 유효.

매력적인 가격, 변하지 않은 주주환원 정책의 방향성 
목표주가와 매수의견 유지. 메모리반도체 주도 폭발적 이익 상승이 가격 (주
가)의 매력을 지속 유발하고 있고, 주주환원 정책의 강화 방향성에도 변한 것
은 없다는 판단. 절대적으로 싼 주가를 비중 확대의 기회로 삼을 필요. 
1. 주주환원, 시점의 문제 
금번 실적발표에 대한 시장의 반응은 다소 냉소적. 대외 불확실성이 큰 만큼, 
시장은 자사주 매입/소각과 같은 즉각적 주가 부양 정책의 발표를 기대.  
당사는 삼성전자의 주주환원이 “시점의 문제”라 생각. 총 주주환원의 규모도, 
차기 (2027-2029년) 주주환원 정책의 강화 방향에도 변한 것은 없다는 판
단. 시장이 기대하는 자사주 매입/소각은 우선주 중심으로 시행될 가능성이 
높으며, 시행 시, 그 규모는 25조원 내외일 것 (DPS 12,567원)으로 전망. 
2. 메모리반도체, 더 올라갈 것 
보수적 환율 전망 (2027년 평균 환율 1,300원 적용)에도, 2027년 메모리반도
체 영업이익은 608조원에 달할 것. 이익 전망의 상향 여력 또한 유효. 
1) 최대 생산, 최대 판매 
삼성전자는 금일 4Q26 DRAM 판매량 가이던스로 전분기 대비 10% 중후반 
성장 (q-q +18%로 추정)을 제시. 일부 투자자들은 높아진 기저 부담으로 
2027년 판매 성장이 제한될 것을 우려. 당사는 높아진 기저에도 2027년 
DRAM 판매가 전년대비 20% 상승할 것으로 전망. 
- 극단적 수급 불균형 속, 삼성전자는 “최대 생산, 최대 판매” 정책을 시행 
중. 삼성전자만의 강력한 Execution이 20% 대 성장을 유지시킬 원동력. 
- DRAM 1c 수율의 조기 개선 속, 범용 DRAM 양산에 DRAM 1c Capa가 일
부 투입될 것으로 예상. Bit 성장을 높일 수 있는 변화가 될 것. 
2) 열려 있는 가격의 상방 
1Q27 모바일 DRAM에서 Surprise가 발생할 것이라는 의견을 유지 (전분기 
대비 30% 이상 상승). 장기계약이 체결된 CSP향으로도 가격의 추가 상승 여
력이 존재한다는 점을 상기할 필요. CSP의 경우, 당장의 비용 부담보다 AI 시
장 선점을 우선시. 장기계약으로 형성된 가격 Band의 천장 (Ceiling) 또한 시
장 우려 대비 낮지 않음을 감안할 필요. 2027년 범용 DRAM ASP는 전년대비 
40% 중후반까지 상승할 가능성 (현 전망: 전년대비 34% 상승).  
3) HBM, 제품 경쟁력과 점유율의 동반 성장  
최근 삼성전자향으로 HBM4/HBM4e 추가 공급 요청이 쇄도하고 있는 것으
로 추정. 일부 고객은 연내 추가 공급 시 2027년 계약 가격을 선제 지급할 
의사를 표출. 제품 경쟁력이 회복되었음을 보여주는 증거라 생각. 
제품 경쟁력의 회복과 최대 Capa의 이점 속, 2027년은 HBM 점유율 1위를 
확보하는 기념비적 해가 될 것으로 전망 (매출액 기준 점유율 38% 예상).

### Page 6

삼성전자(005930)

재무제표

Document 299: Bank of England set for first rate hike as energy shock worsens

Source PDF: all report pdf/Bank of England set for first rate hike as energy shock worsens.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

We expect a 7-2 vote to hike rates this week and, barring a
collapse in energy prices, we think a follow-up move in
February is likely too. However, market pricing for the Bank of
England still looks extreme

James Smith, Michiel Tukker and Chris Turner
The Bank of England is set to raise rates by 25bp to 4% on Thursday
Higher energy prices set to push inﬂation
above 4%
The Bank of England has so far managed to avoid a rate hike since
the start of the Iran war, but that’s about to change. We, like most,
expect a hike at its 5 November meeting, and think a follow-up
move in February is more likely than not.
Why now? Until now, the Bank would argue it’s been on “active
hold”. It was set to cut rates by upwards of 50 basis points this year,
so simply not doing that amounted to de facto tightening. By that
logic, the Bank has added a similar amount of policy restriction as
the European Central Bank, which has lifted its deposit rate twice.

### Page 2

But the worsening energy crisis has made it much harder for the
Bank not to act. The spike in natural gas prices means inﬂation is
set to take another leg higher into winter. A circa 25% rise in the
regulated cap on household energy bills in January is all but baked
in.
We expect headline CPI to peak a touch above 4% early next year,
and we think the BoE's new forecasts will show this too. The Bank’s
July projections had inﬂation reaching 3.2% in late 2026, based on

oil below US$80/bbl and natural gas at around half its current level.
Energy prices have risen signiﬁcantly since
the Bank's July forecasts
*November forecasts based on a three week observation window until 27
October, which is what we assume the BoE will use for its forecasts
Source: Macrobond, ING
The Bank is set to hike rates for insurance
purposes
4% is an important line in the sand for the BoE. Last summer, the
Bank set out research suggesting that second-round eﬀects

become more likely once inﬂation surpasses this level. We suspect
this is still front of mind for many oﬃcials.
Then again, there is essentially no sign of these much-feared
second-round eﬀects so far. Away from motor fuel, inﬂation has
been in line with the Bank’s July forecasts. Our measure of 'energy-

### Page 3

intensive' inﬂation has actually fallen this year, even after
accounting for one-oﬀ changes in water bills and car tax.
One of the Bank’s favoured measures of ‘core services’ inﬂation,
which strips out volatile and indexed categories, is well-behaved
too. And though it has likely bottomed out, private-sector wage
growth at 3.4% year-on-year is hardly problematic. Despite signs of
stabilisation in the jobs market, there’s little evidence wage
pressures are set to build.

True, growth has been much better than feared. But then the Bank
has often taken a more sceptical view of the GDP numbers,
preferring to look at more benign survey-based measures of
activity.
This all leaves the Bank in a strange situation: hiking not because
the economic data is telling it to, but because of the risks presented
by sharply higher energy prices. The energy crisis has reached
suﬃcient depth and longevity that oﬃcials likely feel they,

reluctantly, have to act.
'Energy-intensive' inﬂation hasn't picked up
Based on data from the ONS categorising the inﬂation basket by energy
intensity
Source: Macrobond, ING
The key things to watch
So what should we watch at the November meeting?

### Page 4

First, the vote split. We know there are two doves, Swati Dhingra and
Alan Taylor, who are deeply sceptical of the need for higher rates.
But we suspect the other seven committee members will back a
hike. Could there be more dissenters? Governor Andrew Bailey
hasn’t sounded hugely enthusiastic about hiking, though a
governor has only dissented twice since 1997 and both times in the
ﬁnancial crisis.
So if there is extra dissent, Sarah Breeden looks the most likely

candidate, having stressed that most businesses are absorbing the
energy shock rather than raising prices. Dave Ramsden is another,
less likely, candidate.
Second, there’s the forward guidance. We think the Bank will be as
non-committal as possible. Something like: “The Committee will
carefully assess the extent to which higher energy prices are
propagating into wage and price-setting. The timing and pace of
any further increases will depend on the evolution of the inﬂation

outlook. Monetary policy is not on a pre-set path.”
That’s a clever way of saying: we could hike again, but it’s not
inevitable.
If there’s going to be a hawkish surprise, it would be if the Bank says
something like “some further tightening in monetary policy is likely
to be appropriate over the coming months”.
Another rate hike in February seems to be the growing consensus –
and our own view too. There’s little precedent for ‘one-and-done’
rate rises. But it’s one thing thinking that, another saying it out

loud. There’s a lot that can happen between now and February. And
having made a hawkish misstep all the way back in March, when
the Bank sounded open-minded about tighter policy, opening the
ﬂoodgates for markets to price in rate hikes, it’ll want to tread
carefully here.
A more dovish formulation would be if the Bank makes further hikes
explicitly contingent on second-round eﬀects: one hike as an
insurance move, but anything more requiring solid data. For

example, “If there were evidence of more persistent second-round

### Page 5

eﬀects in wage and price-setting, further tightening in monetary
policy would be required”.
Finally, watch the individual member paragraphs tucked away in
the minutes. Does anyone formally push back against market
pricing, which has more than four hikes priced by this time next
year? Even Chief Economist Huw Pill, one of the hawks, has recently
indicated that such pricing looks unlikely.
The impact on rates markets
Even if the Bank does choose to push back, will markets listen?

A simple model of two-year swap rates against oil, natural gas,
equity markets and the equivalent USD and EUR rates shows that
these global factors are explaining less of the daily movement in
sterling rates than they were over the summer.
Global factors are explaining less of GBP
rates moves
Source: Macrobond, ING
Indeed, the dovish repricing that we’ve seen for the Federal Reserve
and European Central Bank has not been mirrored in the UK.
Perhaps it’s because Britain lacks a story like France has or, in

America’s case, AI fears. But robust UK growth data may also be
playing a role. Economic surprise indices have been touching multi-
year highs.

### Page 6

Still, UK swap rates remain deeply correlated with oil prices. And
barring a big surprise, and with more than 100bp of hikes already
priced, it’s hard to see the Bank triggering a substantial repricing of
rate expectations at this meeting.
GBP rates remain positively correlated with
oil - unlike EUR rates
Source: Macrobond, ING
The impact on the pound
Sterling is trading reasonably well, helped by the view that high
energy prices leave the Bank of England no alternative but to hike

rates on Thursday.
Upside economic surprises, UK ﬁscal policy seemingly heading in
the right direction and warmer ties with the EU all seem to be
helping sterling too. Notably, speculators are still running
reasonably large short positions in sterling, which suggests this
period of sterling outperformance can continue.
EUR/GBP has come a long way very quickly – a near 4% drop in a
little over a month. Some consolidation is probably due. But with
high energy prices likely to keep expectations of higher UK policy

rates in place until early next year and French budgetary problems
unresolved, it is too early to expect a signiﬁcant low in EUR/GBP.

Document 300: Belgium’s budget crisis is serious, but not hopeless

Source PDF: all report pdf/Belgium’s budget crisis is serious, but not hopeless.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-03
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

Belgium's budget talks have hit a wall, but the crisis is not yet
at a dead end. A deal on €10 billion in savings remains our base
case, though political red lines need to soften. A watered-down
compromise would leave debt risks unresolved, while a
government collapse could put investor conﬁdence under
pressure
Philippe Ledent
Belgian Prime Minister Bart De Wever tendered his resignation to the King on
Monday
For several weeks, parties in the Belgian federal government have

been trying to agree on the main budget measures for the next
three years. The aim is to reach an agreement delivering €10 billion
in recurring savings in the federal budget (which also includes social
security). This would not only oﬀset the automatic increase in
expenditure linked to population ageing and interest payments on
the debt, but also reduce the deﬁcit from around 5% of GDP this
year to close to 4% of GDP in 2029.

Conﬁrm selection Accept all

### Page 2

However, last weekend, Prime Minister Bart De Wever had to
acknowledge that the negotiations had failed on two fronts. First,
given the time needed for parliament to debate and vote on the
legislation, it will no longer be possible to start 2027 with a duly
approved budget. Second, there are too many sticking points for a
political agreement to be reached at this stage.
What are the sticking points?
Speciﬁcally, the negotiations have been complicated by the

withdrawal of a key proposal from the Prime Minister. He had
proposed an across-the-board increase in the VAT rate, which would
have raised around €4 billion. Although several parties were
reluctant, one ruled it out altogether, forcing De Wever to take the
proposal oﬀ the negotiating table. Since then, however, other
parties have also set red lines (on social security savings, for
example) or insisted on measures that must be included (notably
broader taxation of ﬁnancial wealth). Finding a compromise has

therefore become more diﬃcult.
Furthermore, the social tensions aﬀecting many European
countries make it harder to agree on austerity measures. Any
spending cut is diﬃcult to implement, but a degree of social
cohesion must be maintained to avoid recreating in Belgium the
severe tensions seen in France. The challenge is therefore to strike
the right balance, which is harder to achieve than it would be
during a period of strong economic growth.
The government remains in ofﬁce for now

Faced with the parties’ conﬂicting red lines and diﬃculties in the
negotiations, De Wever drew the consequences and tendered his
resignation to the King on Monday. To the outside world, this could
seem like the fall of the government – but some perspective is
needed.
First, King Philippe has not yet accepted the Prime Minister’s
resignation. While he does not exercise personal political authority,
he can play an important consultative role at moments of political

uncertainty. He has deferred his decision until Friday. In the
Cookie settings

Targeting Cookies

### Page 3

meantime, the King will meet the leaders of the federal coalition
parties to hear their views and call for a compromise.
Second, by oﬀering his resignation, the Prime Minister probably
wants to send a clear signal to the other parties, warning them that
the price of failed negotiations would be very high. Indeed, the
latest opinion polls show that, if new elections were held, the
parties forming the current majority would not be able to renew it.

The far right would very likely gain ground in the Flemish part of the
country, as would the far left in the French-speaking part. This is
also a signal to voters across the coalition parties. Their party will
have to accept measures that run counter to its programme, but
that is the price to pay to put public ﬁnances on a sound footing,
keep the current coalition together and avoid a political crisis.
Third, the King begins his consultations today, but there have been

numerous political reactions since the weekend. Within the
majority, all have conﬁrmed a willingness to continue negotiations
and called for a sense of responsibility. Nobody wants the
government to fall. In the opposition, one party has even oﬀered its
support to form an alternative majority, which also puts pressure
on some coalition parties to accept compromises within the current
majority.
What is the likely scenario?
It is diﬃcult to predict what will happen. Tensions are high, and

although nobody wants the government to fall, this cannot be
ruled out. Our base case nevertheless remains that, following this
week’s consultations and discussions between the parties, more
time will be granted to reach an agreement on the €10bn in savings
over the next three years. After this week’s political shock, an
agreement would ultimately be reached. It would be too late to
start 2027 with a budget approved by parliament, but the damage
would be relatively limited. A mechanism exists to temporarily

“extend” the 2026 budget until the 2027 budget is duly approved.
If the parties fail to reach an agreement, there are two alternative
scenarios:

Download
1. The parties manage to reach a minimal agreement on a
budget for 2027, involving a smaller ﬁscal eﬀort of around €3-4

billion (the Prime Minister had already proposed this option),
abandoning a multi-year planned eﬀort. However, even if the
government commits to continuing discussions for subsequent
years, it will be diﬃcult to go much further. The government
would then remain in oﬃce, but the eﬀort needed to put public
ﬁnances on a sound footing and make the debt trajectory
sustainable would not be delivered in the short term. Belgium
would gain some breathing space but would remain exposed

to the risk of weaker investor conﬁdence.
2. The deadlock is too severe, leaving the King with no choice but
to accept the government’s resignation. A period of
considerable uncertainty would then begin. Further initiatives
might still be launched to form a new majority, but new
elections would be more likely. The current government would
remain in oﬃce in a caretaker capacity, and the 2026 budget
would be extended into 2027. Without savings measures, the

public deﬁcit would continue to rise, and the election results
could make forming a new coalition very diﬃcult. This scenario
would clearly leave Belgium more exposed to investor distrust.
The situation would closely resemble that currently being faced
in France. To be clear, this scenario cannot be entirely ruled out,
but its probability remains low for now.

France has also seen its debt rise in relative terms. As recently as the GFC in 2008, France’s
debt-to-GDP was basically in line with Germany’s and well below Italy’s, but today it’s far
above Germany’s and closing in on Italy’s. Outside of the temporary Covid spike, Italian
debt/GDP has been stable for over a decade.

### Page 2

The Week Ahead October 23, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         2

CENTRAL BANK MEETINGS

Bank of Thailand (BOT) (October 28)

We expect the BOT to maintain its policy 
rate at 1.00% at its 28 October meeting, 
extending its pause for a fourth 
consecutive decision as it balances 
inflationary pressures against uneven 
and subdued economic growth. Our view 
is aligned with fixed market income 
pricing. Headline inflation rose anew to 
2.8% yoy in September 2026, driven by 
higher energy, food, and core price 
increases, but remained within the upper 
end of the central bank’s 1 -3% target 
range. As the BOT is still likely to look 
through what it views as temporary, 
supply-driven inflationary pressu res, it 
will likely refrain from tightening policy, 
and keep interest rates accommodative 
to support the soft and uneven growth 
cycle.

European Central Bank (October 29)

We expect the ECB to extend its pause, 
keeping the deposit facility rate at 2.5%. 
Market participants will instead focus on 
the central bank’s assessment of rising 
French and Italian bond yields and 
widening spreads versus Bunds. While 
we do not expect dir ect references to 
individual countries, the ECB is likely to 
signal that recent developments in 
France reflect the country’s weaker 
fiscal and debt profile rather than a 
broader systemic concern. Policymakers 
will seek to differentiate between a 
localised bond sell -off and a Eurozone -
wide market disruption, justifying their 
decision to monitor developments rather 
than implement specific measures at this 
juncture. Meanwhile, inflation continues 
to rise, with little relief expected in 
upcoming readings given elevated 
global fuel prices. 
Bank of Japan (October 30)

The Bank of Japan is expected to keep 
the overnight call rate unchanged at 1.25% 
at this meeting, pausing after the 25bp 
hike in September. The BOJ is likely to 
assess the impact of the previous rate 
hike before taking further action. The 
recent stabilisat ion of the yen also 
reduces the urgency of another hike at 
this meeting.

The tightening bias should remain intact. 
Growth and inflation remain broadly on 
track, leaving room for the BOJ to revise 
up its FY2026 –FY2027 GDP and CPI 
forecasts. Wage growth has also 
remained strong, with Rengo reportedly 
targeting wage increases of 6% in 2027, 
reinforcing the BOJ’s confidence that 
underlying inflation can be sustained 
around its 2% target. We continue to 
expect the policy rate to rise to 1.50% in 
December 2026 or January 2027, 
followed by a further increase to 1.75% 
by 2Q 2027.

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

Hong Kong SAR

Export growth is expected to accelerate 
from 53.0% yoy in August to 55.0% in 
September, in line with the pickup in 
China’s export growth from 25.0% to 
36.3%. As a key re-export hub for China,

### Page 3

The Week Ahead October 23, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         3

Hong Kong should benefit from robust 
AI-related electronics trade, with 
electrical machinery, equipment and 
parts exports to Hong Kong accounting 
for 20.6% of China’s total exports. 
Import growth is also expected to remain 
firm, supported by resilient expo rt 
orders and gradually improving 
investment sentiment, sustaining 
demand for intermediate and capital 
goods.

Singapore

Singapore’s industrial production (IP) 
likely remained in expansion for a 13th 
consecutive month in September 2026, 
although we expect growth to moderate 
to 7.6% yoy from August’s strong 15.4% 
yoy pace. IP growth continued to be 
underpinned by the electronics and 
precision engineering clusters, which 
has been key drivers of Singapore’s 
manufacturing strength, reflecting 
sustained demand for artificial 
intelligence-related hardware. However, 
performance across clusters remained 
uneven, with chemicals and biomedical 
manufacturing likely contracting, and 
tempering overall gains.

South Korea

The preliminary estimate for 3Q26 GDP 
is likely to show growth remaining solid 
at 3.2% yoy , slightly below 3.7% in 2Q 
and 3.8% in 1Q. Nominal GDP growth is 
likely to remain in double digits, 
significantly outpacing real GDP growth, 
thanks to improved terms of trade amid 
rising memory export prices. By 
expenditure, exports picked up in 3Q in 
both nominal and real terms, supported 
by stronger semiconductor shipments. 
Investment also appeared robust, as 
reflected in the equipment investment 
estimation index and machinery order 
index. Private consumption, however, 
appeared to weaken amid the sharp 
KOSPI correction since July following  
the introduction of leveraged ETF 
regulations. Our full -year GDP growth 
forecasts of 3.5% for 2026 and 2.4% for 
2027 remain on track.

Taiwan

The preliminary estimate for 3Q GDP is 
likely to show growth remaining strong 
at 12 –13% yoy , close to 12.9% in 2Q, 
albeit below the peak of 15.4% in 1Q. 
Exports maintained strong double -digit 
growth in 3Q (44.6% yoy  in nominal 
terms and 21.1% in real terms), supported 
by robust shipments of semiconductors, 
servers and other AI -related products. 
Machinery investment also sustained 
strong double-digit growth, underpinned 
by higher capacity utilisation in the ICT 
sector and a resulting increase in capital 
goods imports. Private consumption 
appeared to have strengthened further, 
supported by stock market gains, 
improving wage growth, and the 
associated wealth and income effects. 
Construction investment remained the 
weak s pot, weighed down by elevated 
inventories in the new-home market and 
the ongoing need for inventory digestion. 
We maintain our 2026 and 2027 GDP 
growth forecasts at 11.6% and 5.6%, 
respectively, with risks tilted to the 
upside.

### Page 4

### Page 1

#rba #consumer #australia #inflation #housing #houseprices
Why energy costs are yet to come down
By Sian Fenner & Justin Smirk
07:35 October 23 2026 Share
A video with Head of Industry Economics, Sian Fenner, and Senior Economist, Justin
Smirk, on why oil prices have pushed higher and yet, while the risks to the outlook is two
sided, the upside risk presents a more dramatic shift in prices.
Sian and Justin discuss why auto fuel-driven inflation will rise before easing as oil markets normalise.

They examine oil supply risks, Australian fuel prices and inflation plus the implications for the RBA.
Brent crude is expected to average about US$101 in Q4 2026, then fall toward US$77 by Q4 2027,
while and escalation in the conflict could push it to about US$150 in the first half of 2027.

### Page 2

Related articles
The September CPI is forecast to rise 0.6%, lifting annual pace from 4.0 to 4.1%; the December-
quarter CPI is now expected to lift 1.3%, mainly because fuel prices are expected to rise about 15% in
the quarter per cent. Through 2027, falling fuel prices could reduce headline inflation to around 2.4%yr
by year end in 2027, but prolonged conflict could keep all measures of inflation above those levels well
into 2028.

Document 501: 【國泰證期研究部】個股速報-國巨(2327 TT)-營運槓桿顯現，獲利動能延續-20261029

Source PDF: all report pdf/【國泰證期研究部】個股速報-國巨(2327 TT)-營運槓桿顯現，獲利動能延續-20261029.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

國巨 2327 TT  ( 電子–零組件 ) 
營運槓桿顯現，獲利動能延續 
投資與評價 
1. 國巨 3Q26受惠稼動率提升及前次漲價效益反映，預估毛利率 41.1%、營益
率 29.7%，獲利為近八年新高；4Q26預期隨外溢訂單挹注及最新一輪漲價
逐步反映，營收及營業利益將再創歷史新高。 
2. 國巨 26年受惠 AI需求帶動出貨，交期拉長帶動漲價。預期 26年底村田可
能釋出非蘋 MLCC產能，27年隨日系供應商將產能轉向高階 AI MLCC，供
需缺口進一步擴大，預估 26年 EPS 21.21元、27年 EPS 45.70元。 
3. 目前國巨股價為 547元，約為 12X FY27E EPS，位於近三年 11～50倍 PER
參考區間下緣位置。三星電機 3Q26財報正向進展，更多 LTA簽訂並持續與
客戶協商中，且村田持續傳出 EOL消息。展望後市，預期國巨 AI產品占比
將隨整體 AI需求提升，中階產品外溢訂單持續成長，鉭質電容受惠高市占率
及定價能力，惟市場未能適當反應外溢訂單效應與 27 年漲價幅度，故維持
買進投資評等，微調 27年獲利預估，目標價上修至710元(15x FY27E PER)。 
重點更新 
營運槓桿顯現，預期國巨 3Q26獲利為近八年新高 
國巨 3Q26 受惠前次漲價效益開始反映，加上中階外溢訂單挹注帶動稼動率提
升，3Q26 營收為 495.31 億元(+11.4% QoQ)，與預估之 495.57億元(+11.5% 
QoQ)相近，並優於公司財測之溫和成長幅度。在產品組合改善下，調高毛利率預
估至 41.1%(+2.6 ppts QoQ)，營業費用率則降至 11.4%(-0.7 ppts QoQ)，營運
槓桿顯現，預估營業淨利季增 25.1%至 146.95 億元，營益率 29.7%。稅後淨利
預估為 119.45 億元(+26.8% QoQ)，EPS 5.80 元，毛利率連續第五季上揚，獲
利為近八年新高。

預期國巨 4Q26受惠多重成長動能延續，營收再創新高 
4Q26為客戶庫存檢視節點，客戶後續將增加下單，使供需更趨緊俏，進一步支
撐漲價。然而，以中低階 MLCC為主的消費性需求仍低迷，預期最新一輪漲價貢
獻遞延至 4Q26末。預估 4Q26營收為 534.61億元(+7.9% QoQ)，在稼動率持
續提升及產品組合優化下，上修毛利率預期至 44.2%(+3.1 ppts QoQ)。預期營
業淨利為 175.20億元(+19.2% QoQ)，稅後淨利將季增 19.7%達 142.95億元，
EPS 6.94元，受惠多重成長動能，營收及營業利益將再創歷史新高。

買進 – 維持買進 
目標價：710元  +29.7% 
收盤價：547元

中国の成長分野における赤字企業の増加
中国では、 赤字企業が増加している。 とりわけ、 成長分野における増加が顕著である。 この要因の一つは、
し烈な企業間競争による選別である。また、赤字でも潜在力が評価される次世の成長企業も多い。
■成長分野における赤字企業の増加
近年、中国では成長分野における赤字企業の
増加が顕著である。上場企業数に占める赤字企
業の比率（赤字企業比率）は2019年の 9.6％か
ら 2025年に 26.9％へ急上昇している （右上図）。
赤字企業の業種分布をみると、2019～22 年は、
新型コロナウイルス対策として実施された厳格
な外出規制や不動産不況を受けて、飲食・宿泊
業上場企業（以下、上場企業を省略）や不動産
業など非製造業を中心に増加した。 一方で、2022
～25 年には、情報通信機器・電子部品・デバイ
ス、はん用・生産用・業務用機械、電気機械、

化学工業で赤字企業が急増している。これら 4
分野だけで全体の上昇分の約6割を説明できる。
これらの分野は中国製造業の代表的な成長分
野である。実際、この 4 分野の付加価値額の増
加率は、いずれも中国製造業の平均値を上回る。
中国政府が重点的に育成してきたハイテク製造
業も、 これらの分野に多数含まれる。 たとえば、
5G基地局や半導体は、 情報通信機器・電子部品・
デバイスに分類される。ロボットは、はん用・
生産用・業務用機械に含まれる。太陽電池や蓄
電池は電気機械、バイオ医薬品や有機化学素材
は化学工業に属している。
情報通信機器・電子部品・デバイス製造業の
近年の動きをみると、2019年頃から世界的に通信規格が4G（LTE）から 5G サービスへの移行
がみられ、2022年 11 月に ChatGPTが公開された。これらが、同分野の成長の追い風となって
いる。 足元では、 一般の消費者や企業がAIサービスを日常的に利用できることが示されたことで、
AI向け端末や半導体、 データセンターの需要が急増している。 こうしたなか、 多くの企業が開発・

製造・販売の強化に注力しており、 同分野の付加価値額は2022年の7,800億元から2025年に9,100
億元へと増加し続けている。その一方で、赤字企業数は同期間に113社から 198社へ増加し、赤
字企業比率は17.2％から 30.1％へと急上昇している（右下図）。
■企業間競争を通じた選別
成長分野でありながら赤字企業が増加している理由の一つは、し烈な企業間競争である。市場
シェアを巡って、設備投資競争や価格競争が過熱している。情報通信機器・電子部品・デバイス
主任研究員 関 辰一
(seki.shinichi@jri.co.jp）
